   1++6E58              ; ============================================================================
   2++6E58              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6E58              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6E58              ;
   5++6E58              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6E58              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6E58              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6E58              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6E58              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6E58              ; ============================================================================
  11++6E58              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6E58              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6E58              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6E58              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6E58              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6E58              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6E58
  18++6E58              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6E58                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6E58 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6E5B 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6E5E B7                   or      a
  23++6E5F ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6E61 C2 A0 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6E64
  26++6E64                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6E64 21 A0 93             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6E67 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6E6A B7                   or      a
  30++6E6B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6E6D DA A0 6E             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6E70 CA A0 6E             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6E73 22 BA 6E             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6E76 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6E77 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6E79 D2 A0 6E             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6E7C
  38++6E7C 21 A0 93             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6E7F 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6E82 B7                   or      a
  41++6E83 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6E85 DA 8E 6E             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6E88 CA 8E 6E             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6E8B C3 A0 6E             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6E8E              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6E8E                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6E8E 3A 4D 77             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6E91 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6E93 C2 A0 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6E96 3A 4A 77             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6E99 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6E9B C2 A0 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6E9E B7                   or      a
  54++6E9F C9                   ret
  55++6EA0
  56++6EA0              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6EA0 37                   scf
  58++6EA1 C9                   ret
  59++6EA2
  60++6EA2              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6EA2              p127_layout_report:
  62++6EA2 21 BC 6E             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6EA5 CD 06 63             call    p127_puts
  64++6EA8 3A BB 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6EAB CD 22 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6EAE 3A BA 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6EB1 CD 22 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6EB4 21 D8 6E             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6EB7 C3 06 63             jp      p127_puts
  70++6EBA
  71++6EBA              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6EBA 00 00                dw      0
  73++6EBC              p127_msg_kernel_size:

  74++6EC0 37 5D 20 4B

 454+ 6EF4                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6EF4              ; ============================================================================
   2++6EF4              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6EF4              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6EF4              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6EF4              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6EF4              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6EF4              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6EF4              ; ============================================================================
   9++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6EF4
  13++6EF4              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6EF4              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6EF4              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6EF4              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6EF4              p127_resident_start:
  18++6EF4
  19++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6EF4              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6EF4
  26++6EF4              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6EF4              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6EF4              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6EF4              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6EF4              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6EF4 32 7E 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6EF7 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6EF8 32 7F 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6EFB 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6EFC 32 80 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6EFF 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6F00 32 81 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6F03
  39++6F03 3A 7E 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6F06 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6F08 C2 32 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6F0B 3A 7F 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6F0E FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6F10 C2 32 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6F13 3A 80 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6F16 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6F18 C2 32 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6F1B 3A 81 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6F1E E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6F20 CA 32 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6F23                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6F23 3A 7E 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6F26 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6F28 CA 32 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6F2B FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6F2D CA 32 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6F30 B7                   or      a
  58++6F31 C9                   ret
  59++6F32
  60++6F32              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6F32 37                   scf
  62++6F33 C9                   ret
  63++6F34
  64++6F34              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6F34              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6F34              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6F34 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6F36 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6F38 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6F3A 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6F3C CD F4 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6F3F DA 7C 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6F42
  74++6F42 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6F44 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6F46 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6F48 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6F4A CD F4 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6F4D D2 7C 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6F50
  81++6F50 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6F52 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6F54 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6F56 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6F58 CD F4 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6F5B D2 7C 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6F5E
  88++6F5E 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6F60 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6F62 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6F64 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6F66 CD F4 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6F69 D2 7C 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6F6C
  95++6F6C 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6F6E 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6F70 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6F72 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6F74 CD F4 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6F77 D2 7C 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++6F7A B7                   or      a
 102++6F7B C9                   ret

 105++6F7C 37                   scf
 106++6F7D C9                   ret

 108++6F7E              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++6F7E 00                   db      0

 348++7125              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++7125              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7125              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7125              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7125              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7125              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7125              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7125              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++7125              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:

 362++712D ED 52                sbc     hl,de

 368++7139 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++713B D2 72 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail

 374++7144 B7                   or      a
 375++7145 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++7147 DA 72 71             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++714A

 379++714D 11 74 71             ld      de,p127_resident_end
 380++7150 B7                   or      a
 381++7151 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++7153 D2 72 71             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++7156

 385++7156                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++7156 21 00 00             ld      hl,0

   1++7174              ; ============================================================================
   2++7174              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++7174              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++7174              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++7174              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++7174              ; ============================================================================
   7++7174              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++7174              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++7174              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++7174              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++7174              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++7174              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++7174
  14++7174              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++7174              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++7174              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++7174              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++7174              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++7174              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++7174              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++7174              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++7174              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++7174              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++7174              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++7174
  26++7174              p127_irq_init:
  27++7174 F3                   di
  28++7175 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++7177 32 57 74             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++717A AF                   xor     a
  31++717B 32 58 74             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++717E 32 59 74             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++7181 32 5A 74             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++7184 B7                   or      a
  35++7185 C9                   ret
  36++7186
  37++7186              ; OUT: carry clear only while the documented DI-only/no-vector contract holds.
  38++7186              p127_irq_validate:

  45++7196 3A 59 74             ld      a,(p127_irq_mode_policy)
  46++7199 FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET
  47++719B C2 DE 72             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  48++719E B7                   or      a
  49++719F C9                   ret

 239++72E5              ; Stage 2.15: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 240++72E5              ; Hardware vector installation and IM selection are deliberately separate.
 241++72E5              ; Entry contract: CPU has already pushed the interrupted PC; W1/P5 code and
 242++72E5              ; W2/P2 stack remain mapped. The dispatcher may freely clobber all registers.
 243++72E5              ; RETI restores IFF1 from IFF2; this routine never executes EI or IM.
 244++72E5              p127_irq_entry:

   1++745B              ; ============================================================================
   2++745B              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++745B              ; Module version: 0.4.4
   4++745B              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++745B              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++745B              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++745B              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++745B              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++745B              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++745B              ; ============================================================================
  11++745B              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0

   2++77C8              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++77C8              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++77C8              ; ABI:
   5++77C8              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++77C8              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++77C8              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++77C8              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++77C8              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++77C8              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.

  13++77C8              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

 458+ 78E2                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_arena.asm"
# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++78E2              ; ============================================================================
   2++78E2              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++78E2              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++78E2              ;
   5++78E2              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++78E2              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++78E2              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++78E2              ;
   9++78E2              ; API:
  10++78E2              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++78E2              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++78E2              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++78E2              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++78E2              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++78E2              ;
  16++78E2              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++78E2              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++78E2              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++78E2              ; ============================================================================
  20++78E2              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++78E2              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++78E2              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++78E2              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++78E2              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++78E2
  26++78E2              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++78E2              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++78E2              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++78E2              p127_arena_init:
  30++78E2 21 87 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++78E5 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++78E7 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  33++78E9              .p127_arena_init_scan:
  34++78E9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  35++78EA B7                   or      a
  36++78EB C2 F5 78             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++78EE 2B                   dec     hl
  38++78EF 0D                   dec     c
  39++78F0 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++78F2 C3 A8 79             jp      p127_arena_reject

   1++7A40              ; ============================================================================
   2++7A40              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7A40              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7A40              ;
   5++7A40              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7A40              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7A40              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7A40              ;
   9++7A40              ; API:
  10++7A40              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7A40              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7A40              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7A40              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7A40              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7A40              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7A40              ; ============================================================================
  17++7A40              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7A40              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7A40              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0

   5++7CE1              ; Metadata only: no byte access/copy, mapping, OUT, DMA, IRQ, IM or EI.

   1++7EC1              ; ============================================================================
   2++7EC1              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7EC1              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7EC1              ;
   5++7EC1              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7EC1              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7EC1              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7EC1              ;
   9++7EC1              ; API inputs:
  10++7EC1              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7EC1              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7EC1              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7EC1              ; Outputs:
  14++7EC1              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7EC1              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7EC1              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7EC1              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7EC1              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7EC1              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7EC1              ; ============================================================================
  21++7EC1              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7EC1              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7EC1              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7EC1              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7EC1              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7EC1
  27++7EC1              p127_as_copy:
  28++7EC1 AF                   xor     a
  29++7EC2 32 C0 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
  30++7EC5 32 BC 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed),a
  31++7EC8 32 BD 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+1),a
  32++7ECB 32 BE 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+2),a
  33++7ECE 32 BF 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+3),a
  34++7ED1
  35++7ED1                      ; Validate source and destination completely before taking W3.
  36++7ED1 21 B0 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source
  37++7ED4 11 AD 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  38++7ED7 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  39++7EDA ED B0                ldir
  40++7EDC 21 B8 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_length
  41++7EDF 11 B1 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_length
  42++7EE2 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  43++7EE5 ED B0                ldir
  44++7EE7 CD E1 7C             call    p127_as_span_check
  45++7EEA DA D3 80             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  46++7EED
  47++7EED 21 B4 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_destination
  48++7EF0 11 AD 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  49++7EF3 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  50++7EF6 ED B0                ldir
  51++7EF8 CD E1 7C             call    p127_as_span_check
  52++7EFB DA D3 80             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  53++7EFE
  54++7EFE                      ; Empty spans are valid no-ops, including an address exactly 4 MiB.
  55++7EFE 3A B8 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  56++7F01 47                   ld      b,a
  57++7F02 3A B9 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+1)
  58++7F05 B0                   or      b
  59++7F06 47                   ld      b,a
  60++7F07 3A BA 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+2)
  61++7F0A B0                   or      b
  62++7F0B 47                   ld      b,a
  63++7F0C 3A BB 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+3)

 177++856F              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++856F              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++856F              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++856F              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++856F              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++856F              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++856F              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++856F              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++856F              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++856F                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++856F                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++856F 5F                   ld      e,a
 189++8570 AF                   xor     a
 190++8571 32 30 86             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 191++8574 78                   ld      a,b
 192++8575 FE 40                cp      64
 193++8577 D2 33 86             jp      nc,p127_map_handoff_reject
 194++857A 7B                   ld      a,e
 195++857B FE 04                cp      P127_MAP_WINDOW_COUNT
 196++857D D2 33 86             jp      nc,p127_map_handoff_reject
 197++8580 16 00                ld      d,0
 198++8582 21 2C 86             ld      hl,p127_map_window_mask
 199++8585 19                   add     hl,de
 200++8586 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 201++8587 A1                   and     c
 202++8588 C2 33 86             jp      nz,p127_map_handoff_reject

   1++8765              ; ============================================================================
   2++8765              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++8765              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8765              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++8765              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++8765              ; ============================================================================
   7++8765              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++8765              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++8765              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++8765
  11++8765              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++8765              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++8765              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++8765              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++8765              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++8765              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++8765              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++8765
  19++8765              ; IN: none.
  20++8765              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++8765              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++8765              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++8765              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++8765              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++8765              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++8765              p127_handoff_stack_probe:

  60++8845              ; accepts a zero-based sector in E and internally selects physical sector E+1.

   1++8D4D              ; ============================================================================
   2++8D4D              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8D4D              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8D4D              ;
   5++8D4D              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8D4D              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8D4D              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8D4D              ;
   9++8D4D              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8D4D              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8D4D              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8D4D              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8D4D              ;
  14++8D4D              ; API:
  15++8D4D              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8D4D              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8D4D              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8D4D              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8D4D              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8D4D              ;
  21++8D4D              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8D4D              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8D4D              ; is assumed.
  24++8D4D              ; ============================================================================
  25++8D4D              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8D4D              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8D4D              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8D4D
  29++8D4D              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8D4D              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8D4D              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8D4D
  33++8D4D              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8D4D E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8D4F 32 AB 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8D52 C9                   ret
  37++8D53
  38++8D53              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8D53              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8D53              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8D53              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8D53 C5                   push    bc
  43++8D54 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8D55 3A AB 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8D58 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8D5A 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8D5B 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8D5C E6 01                and     1
  49++8D5E 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8D60 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8D61 F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8D63 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8D65              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8D65 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8D66              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8D66 32 AB 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8D69 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8D6B C1                   pop     bc
  59++8D6C C9                   ret
  60++8D6D
  61++8D6D              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8D6D              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8D6D 3A AB 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8D70 EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8D72 32 AB 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8D75 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8D77 C9                   ret
  68++8D78
  69++8D78              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8D78              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8D78 3A AB 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8D7B E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8D7D 32 AB 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8D80 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8D82 C9                   ret
  76++8D83
  77++8D83              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8D83 3A AB 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8D86 C9                   ret
  80++8D87
  81++8D87
  82++8D87              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8D87              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8D87              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8D87              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8D87              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8D87 F5                   push    af
  88++8D88 C5                   push    bc
  89++8D89 D5                   push    de
  90++8D8A E5                   push    hl
  91++8D8B E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8D8D CD 4D 8D             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8D90 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8D93              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8D93 CD 6D 8D             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8D96 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8D98              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8D98 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8D9A 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8D9C              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8D9C 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8D9E 1B                   dec     de
 103++8D9F 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8DA0 B3                   or      e
 105++8DA1 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8DA3 CD 78 8D             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8DA6 E1                   pop     hl
 108++8DA7 D1                   pop     de
 109++8DA8 C1                   pop     bc
 110++8DA9 F1                   pop     af
 111++8DAA C9                   ret
 112++8DAB
 113++8DAB 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0
 114++8DAC

  14+ A000              P126_VIDEO_PAGE3 EQU #7C
  15+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE5 EQU #7A
  16+ A000              P126_HOST_PAGE0 EQU #7F
  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80
  18+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_HEIGHT EQU 25
  19+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_FRAME_SIZE EQU 4000
  20+ A000              P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT EQU #00EF
  21+ A000
  22+ A000 06           p126_atm_video_mode: db P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
  23+ A001 00           p126_atm_video_enabled: db 0
  24+ A002 00           Console_X: db 0
  25+ A003 0C           Console_Y: db BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
  26+ A004 00           bios_dbg_row: db 0
  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0
  28+ A006 00           bios_dbg_char: db 0
  29+ A007 00           bios_dbg_write_row: db 0
  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0
  31+ A009 00           bios_dbg_parity: db 0
  32+ A00A 00           bios_dbg_attr: db 0
  33+ A00B 00 00        bios_dbg_offset: dw 0
  34+ A00D 00 00        bios_dbg_text_ptr: dw 0
  35+ A00F 00           bios_dbg_print_row: db 0
  36+ A010 00           bios_dbg_print_col: db 0

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret
 150+ A09B

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.

 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc

 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc

 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)

 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl

 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a

 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de

 516+ A30F 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 517+ A312 ED 79                out     (c), a

 598+ A40C
 599+ A40C
 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2
 610+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA      EQU #C0
 611+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT     EQU #C6
 612+ A40C
 613+ A40C              p127_video_mode_selftest:
 614+ A40C                      ; Preserve representative character and attribute bytes from both
 615+ A40C                      ; parity planes. The mode changes themselves must not alter VRAM.
 616+ A40C AF                   xor     a
 617+ A40D 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 618+ A410 CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 619+ A413 D8                   ret     c
 620+ A414
 621+ A414 3E C3                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR
 622+ A416 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 623+ A419 D8                   ret     c
 624+ A41A CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 625+ A41D
 626+ A41D 3E C2                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640
 627+ A41F CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 628+ A422 D8                   ret     c
 629+ A423 CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 630+ A426
 631+ A426 3E C0                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA
 632+ A428 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 633+ A42B D8                   ret     c
 634+ A42C CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 635+ A42F
 636+ A42F                      ; Always return the controller to the Project127 UI mode.
 637+ A42F 3E C6                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
 638+ A431 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 639+ A434 D8                   ret     c
 640+ A435
 641+ A435 3E 01                ld      a,1
 642+ A437 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 643+ A43A CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 644+ A43D D8                   ret     c
 645+ A43E 3A C3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_even)
 646+ A441 21 C7 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_even
 647+ A444 BE                   cp      (hl)
 648+ A445 20 2D                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 649+ A447 3A C4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_odd)
 650+ A44A 21 C8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_odd
 651+ A44D BE                   cp      (hl)
 652+ A44E 20 24                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 653+ A450 3A C5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_even)
 654+ A453 21 C9 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_even
 655+ A456 BE                   cp      (hl)
 656+ A457 20 1B                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 657+ A459 3A C6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_odd)
 658+ A45C 21 CA A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd
 659+ A45F BE                   cp      (hl)
 660+ A460 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 661+ A462                      ; Compare complete-page CRCs: P7 character plane and P3 attributes.
 662+ A462 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 663+ A465 11 DC A5             ld      de,p127_video_crc_after
 664+ A468 06 04                ld      b,4
 665+ A46A              p127_video_crc_compare_loop:
 666+ A46A 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 667+ A46B BE                   cp      (hl)
 668+ A46C 20 06                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 669+ A46E 23                   inc     hl
 670+ A46F 13                   inc     de
 671+ A470 10 F8                djnz    p127_video_crc_compare_loop
 672+ A472 B7                   or      a
 673+ A473 C9                   ret
 674+ A474
 675+ A474              p127_video_test_fail:
 676+ A474 37                   scf
 677+ A475 C9                   ret
 678+ A476
 679+ A476              ; IN A=complete ATM mode-register byte. Carry means W3 UI lease refused.
 680+ A476              p127_video_switch_mode:
 681+ A476 32 C1 A5             ld      (p127_video_mode_arg),a
 682+ A479 C5                   push    bc
 683+ A47A D5                   push    de
 684+ A47B E5                   push    hl
 685+ A47C CD 92 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 686+ A47F 38 6A                jr      c,p127_video_switch_refused
 687+ A481 CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 688+ A484
 689+ A484                      ; Preserve the original P3 bytes occupied by the temporary trampoline.
 690+ A484                      ; The trampoline returns to resident code, where these bytes are restored
 691+ A484                      ; before P3 is unmapped. This keeps the full-page CRC genuinely read-only.
 692+ A484 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 693+ A487 11 CB A5             ld      de,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 694+ A48A 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 695+ A48D ED B0                ldir
 696+ A48F
 697+ A48F                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p127_video_mode_return.
 698+ A48F 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 699+ A492 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 700+ A494 23                   inc     hl
 701+ A495 36 77                ld      (hl),#77
 702+ A497 23                   inc     hl
 703+ A498 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 704+ A49A 23                   inc     hl
 705+ A49B 36 ED                ld      (hl),#ED
 706+ A49D 23                   inc     hl
 707+ A49E 36 79                ld      (hl),#79
 708+ A4A0 23                   inc     hl
 709+ A4A1 36 C3                ld      (hl),#C3
 710+ A4A3 23                   inc     hl
 711+ A4A4 36 AF                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return & #FF
 712+ A4A6 23                   inc     hl
 713+ A4A7 36 A4                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return >> 8
 714+ A4A9 3A C1 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_mode_arg)
 715+ A4AC C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 716+ A4AF
 717+ A4AF              p127_video_mode_return:
 718+ A4AF                      ; P3 is still mapped here: restore the bytes overwritten by the trampoline.
 719+ A4AF 21 CB A5             ld      hl,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 720+ A4B2 11 EC FF             ld      de,#FFEC
 721+ A4B5 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 722+ A4B8 ED B0                ldir
 723+ A4BA CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 724+ A4BD CD AD 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 725+ A4C0
 726+ A4C0                      ; Verify the CPU windows and W3 ownership contract after EVERY mode
 727+ A4C0                      ; write. W2 must be the host page P5, W3 must be P0, and the UI lease
 728+ A4C0                      ; must be released before another mode transition is attempted.
 729+ A4C0 3A 3A 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+1)
 730+ A4C3 FE 05                cp      5
 731+ A4C5 20 1F                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 732+ A4C7 3A 3C 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 733+ A4CA B7                   or      a
 734+ A4CB 20 19                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 735+ A4CD 3A 1F 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 736+ A4D0 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 737+ A4D2 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 738+ A4D4 3A 20 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 739+ A4D7 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 740+ A4D9 20 0B                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 741+ A4DB 3A 21 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 742+ A4DE B7                   or      a
 743+ A4DF 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 744+ A4E1 E1                   pop     hl
 745+ A4E2 D1                   pop     de
 746+ A4E3 C1                   pop     bc
 747+ A4E4 B7                   or      a
 748+ A4E5 C9                   ret
 749+ A4E6
 750+ A4E6              p127_video_mode_restore_error:
 751+ A4E6 E1                   pop     hl
 752+ A4E7 D1                   pop     de
 753+ A4E8 C1                   pop     bc
 754+ A4E9 37                   scf
 755+ A4EA C9                   ret
 756+ A4EB
 757+ A4EB              p127_video_switch_refused:
 758+ A4EB E1                   pop     hl
 759+ A4EC D1                   pop     de
 760+ A4ED C1                   pop     bc
 761+ A4EE 37                   scf
 762+ A4EF C9                   ret
 763+ A4F0
 764+ A4F0              ; Capture bytes from both character parity planes and both attribute planes.
 765+ A4F0              ; OUT: before signature when p127_video_capture_target=0, after when =1.
 766+ A4F0              p127_video_capture_signature:
 767+ A4F0 C5                   push    bc
 768+ A4F1 D5                   push    de
 769+ A4F2 E5                   push    hl
 770+ A4F3 CD 92 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_enter

 773+ A4FB 3A C2 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_capture_phase)
 774+ A4FE B7                   or      a
 775+ A4FF 20 1A                jr      nz,p127_video_capture_after
 776+ A501 3A C0 41             ld      a,(#41C0)
 777+ A504 32 C3 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_char_even),a
 778+ A507 3A C0 61             ld      a,(#61C0)
 779+ A50A 32 C4 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_char_odd),a
 780+ A50D 3A C0 E1             ld      a,(#E1C0)
 781+ A510 32 C5 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_attr_even),a

 784+ A519 18 18                jr      p127_video_capture_done
 785+ A51B              p127_video_capture_after:
 786+ A51B 3A C0 41             ld      a,(#41C0)
 787+ A51E 32 C7 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_char_even),a
 788+ A521 3A C0 61             ld      a,(#61C0)
 789+ A524 32 C8 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_char_odd),a
 790+ A527 3A C0 E1             ld      a,(#E1C0)
 791+ A52A 32 C9 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_attr_even),a

 793+ A530 32 CA A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd),a
 794+ A533              p127_video_capture_done:
 795+ A533                      ; Full-page CRC-16/CCITT over the complete 16 KiB P7 and P3 windows.
 796+ A533                      ; This is read-only: no VRAM byte is overwritten by the test.
 797+ A533 CD 67 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_pages
 798+ A536 3A C2 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_capture_phase)
 799+ A539 B7                   or      a
 800+ A53A 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_crc_store_after
 801+ A53C 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 802+ A53F 18 03                jr      p127_video_crc_store
 803+ A541              p127_video_crc_store_after:
 804+ A541 21 DC A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_after
 805+ A544              p127_video_crc_store:
 806+ A544 3A D4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p7)
 807+ A547 77                   ld      (hl),a
 808+ A548 23                   inc     hl
 809+ A549 3A D5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p7+1)
 810+ A54C 77                   ld      (hl),a
 811+ A54D 23                   inc     hl
 812+ A54E 3A D6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p3)
 813+ A551 77                   ld      (hl),a
 814+ A552 23                   inc     hl
 815+ A553 3A D7 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p3+1)
 816+ A556 77                   ld      (hl),a
 817+ A557 CD 06 A3             call    p126_screen_restore_host_pages
 818+ A55A CD AD 6F             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 819+ A55D E1                   pop     hl
 820+ A55E D1                   pop     de
 821+ A55F C1                   pop     bc
 822+ A560 B7                   or      a
 823+ A561 C9                   ret
 824+ A562              p127_video_capture_refused:
 825+ A562 E1                   pop     hl
 826+ A563 D1                   pop     de
 827+ A564 C1                   pop     bc
 828+ A565 37                   scf
 829+ A566 C9                   ret
 830+ A567
 831+ A567              ; Calculate CRC-16/CCITT (poly #1021, init #FFFF) over both mapped
 832+ A567              ; 16 KiB pages. W1=#4000..#7FFF is P7; W3=#C000..#FFFF is P3.
 833+ A567              ; OUT: p127_video_crc_p7 and p127_video_crc_p3, low byte first.
 834+ A567              p127_video_crc16_pages:
 835+ A567 C5                   push    bc
 836+ A568 D5                   push    de
 837+ A569 E5                   push    hl
 838+ A56A 21 00 40             ld      hl,#4000
 839+ A56D CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 840+ A570 ED 53 D4 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p7),de
 841+ A574 21 00 C0             ld      hl,#C000
 842+ A577 CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 843+ A57A ED 53 D6 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p3),de
 844+ A57E E1                   pop     hl
 845+ A57F D1                   pop     de
 846+ A580 C1                   pop     bc
 847+ A581 C9                   ret
 848+ A582
 849+ A582              ; IN HL=window base. OUT DE=CRC-16/CCITT.
 850+ A582              p127_video_crc16_page:
 851+ A582 11 FF FF             ld      de,#FFFF
 852+ A585 01 00 40             ld      bc,#4000
 853+ A588              p127_video_crc16_byte:
 854+ A588 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 855+ A589 AA                   xor     d
 856+ A58A 57                   ld      d,a
 857+ A58B 3E 08                ld      a,8
 858+ A58D 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 859+ A590              p127_video_crc16_bit:
 860+ A590 CB 23                sla     e
 861+ A592 CB 12                rl      d
 862+ A594 30 08                jr      nc,p127_video_crc16_no_poly
 863+ A596 7A                   ld      a,d
 864+ A597 EE 10                xor     #10
 865+ A599 57                   ld      d,a
 866+ A59A 7B                   ld      a,e
 867+ A59B EE 21                xor     #21
 868+ A59D 5F                   ld      e,a
 869+ A59E              p127_video_crc16_no_poly:
 870+ A59E 3A D3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_bits)
 871+ A5A1 3D                   dec     a
 872+ A5A2 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 873+ A5A5 20 E9                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_bit
 874+ A5A7 23                   inc     hl
 875+ A5A8 0B                   dec     bc
 876+ A5A9 78                   ld      a,b
 877+ A5AA B1                   or      c
 878+ A5AB 20 DB                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_byte
 879+ A5AD C9                   ret
 880+ A5AE
 881+ A5AE              ; Visible dwell without IRQ/PIT dependencies (IM/EI remain disabled).

   1+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A5E0              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A5E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A5E0              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A5E0              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A5E0              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A5E0
  15+ A5E0              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A5E0              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A5E0              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A5E0              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A5E0              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A5E0              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A5E0              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:

 350+ A8A9 C3 C2 A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error

 402+ A904 5F                   ld      e,a

 420+ A924              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:

 452+ A958              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A958              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.

 456+ A958                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.

 575+ AA94
 576+ AA94              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ AA94              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ AA94              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ AA94 21 48 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ AA97 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ AA99 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ AA9B              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ AA9B 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ AA9C FE 06                cp      6
 585+ AA9E 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ AAA0 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ AAA2 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ AAA4 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ AAA6 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ AAA8 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ AAAA 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ AAAC FE 02                cp      2
 593+ AAAE 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ AAB0 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ AAB2 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ AAB4 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ AAB6 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ AAB8 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ AABA 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ AABC 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ AABE              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ AABE C5                   push    bc
 603+ AABF E5                   push    hl
 604+ AAC0 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ AAC1 16 00                ld      d,0

 607+ AAC6 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ AAC7 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ AAC8 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ AACA E1                   pop     hl
 611+ AACB C1                   pop     bc
 612+ AACC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ AACE              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ AACE 0C                   inc     c
 615+ AACF 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 617+ AAD2 C9                   ret
 618+ AAD3              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ AAD3 37                   scf
 620+ AAD4 C9                   ret
 621+ AAD5
 622+ AAD5              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ AAD5              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ AAD5 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ AAD8 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ AAD9 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ AADC              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:

 632+ AAE1 E5                   push    hl

 637+ AAE9 E1                   pop     hl

 644+ AAF2              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ AAF2 37                   scf
 646+ AAF3 C9                   ret
 647+ AAF4
 648+ AAF4              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ AAF4              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ AAF4              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ AAF4 21 48 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ AAF7 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ AAF9 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ AAFB              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ AAFB 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ AAFC FE 00                cp      0
 657+ AAFE 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ AB00 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ AB02 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ AB04 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ AB06 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ AB08 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ AB0A 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ AB0C FE 04                cp      4
 665+ AB0E 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ AB10 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ AB12 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ AB14 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ AB16 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ AB18 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ AB19 B7                   or      a
 672+ AB1A 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ AB1C 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ AB1E              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ AB1E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ AB1F FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ AB21 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ AB23              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ AB23 23                   inc     hl
 680+ AB24 0C                   inc     c
 681+ AB25 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ AB27 B7                   or      a
 683+ AB28 C9                   ret
 684+ AB29              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ AB29 37                   scf
 686+ AB2A C9                   ret
 687+ AB2B
 688+ AB2B              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ AB2B              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ AB2B
 691+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 692+ AB2B              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ AB2B              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ AB2B              ; Public ABI:
 695+ AB2B              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ AB2B              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ AB2B              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ AB2B              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ AB2B              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ AB2B              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 702+ AB2B              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ AB2B C3 E8 74             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ AB2E
 705+ AB2E              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ AB2E 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ AB31 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ AB34 B7                   or      a
 709+ AB35 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ AB38 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ AB3B 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ AB3C 3A 3C 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ AB3F B8                   cp      b
 714+ AB40 CA E8 AB             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ AB43 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ AB46 C3 FE 74             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ AB49
 718+ AB49              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ AB49              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ AB49 F3                   di
 721+ AB4A 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ AB4D B7                   or      a
 723+ AB4E C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ AB51 3A 3C 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ AB54 B7                   or      a
 726+ AB55 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ AB58 CD B2 6F             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ AB5B D8                   ret     c
 729+ AB5C 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ AB5E 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ AB61 CD D1 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ AB64 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ AB66 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ AB68 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ AB6B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ AB6E 32 8A AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ AB71 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ AB73 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ AB76 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ AB79 B7                   or      a
 741+ AB7A C9                   ret
 742+ AB7B              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ AB7B CD F9 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ AB7E AF                   xor     a
 745+ AB7F 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ AB82 37                   scf
 747+ AB83 C9                   ret
 748+ AB84
 749+ AB84              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ AB84              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ AB84 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ AB87 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ AB8A FE 01                cp      1
 754+ AB8C C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ AB8F 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ AB92 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ AB94 D2 E8 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ AB97 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ AB98 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ AB9A 21 48 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ AB9D 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ AB9E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ AB9F FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ ABA1 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ ABA4 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ ABA7 C3 14 AD             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ ABAA
 768+ ABAA              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ ABAA              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ ABAA              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ ABAA 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ ABAD FE 01                cp      1
 773+ ABAF C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ ABB2 AF                   xor     a
 775+ ABB3 CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ ABB6 D8                   ret     c
 777+ ABB7 3A 89 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ ABBA B7                   or      a
 779+ ABBB 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ ABBD 3A 8A AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ ABC0 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ ABC3              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ ABC3 3A 88 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ ABC6 B7                   or      a
 785+ ABC7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ ABC9 CD E5 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ ABCC D8                   ret     c
 788+ ABCD AF                   xor     a
 789+ ABCE 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ ABD1              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ ABD1 3A 87 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ ABD4 B7                   or      a
 793+ ABD5 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ ABD7 CD F9 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ ABDA D8                   ret     c
 796+ ABDB AF                   xor     a
 797+ ABDC 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ ABDF              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ ABDF AF                   xor     a
 800+ ABE0 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ ABE3 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ ABE6 B7                   or      a
 803+ ABE7 C9                   ret
 804+ ABE8
 805+ ABE8              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ ABE8 37                   scf
 807+ ABE9 C9                   ret
 808+ ABEA
 809+ ABEA              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ ABEA              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ ABEA              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ ABEA AF                   xor     a
 813+ ABEB 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ ABEE 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ ABF1 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ ABF4 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ ABF7 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ ABF9 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ ABFC D2 84 AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ ABFF CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AC02 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AC05 32 8C AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AC08 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AC0B 32 8D AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AC0E CD 49 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AC11 DA 7E AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AC14                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AC14 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AC16 CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AC19 D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AC1C 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AC1F CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AC22 DA 7B AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AC25                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AC25 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AC28 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AC2B D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AC2E 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AC30 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AC33 CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AC36 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AC39 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AC3C 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AC3D 3A 8D AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AC40 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AC41 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AC44 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AC47 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AC4A DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AC4D                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AC4D CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AC50 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AC53 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AC54 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AC57 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AC58 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AC5B 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AC5C CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AC5F DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AC62 3A 1F 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AC65 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AC67 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AC6A 3A 20 71             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AC6D FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AC6F C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AC72 3A 3C 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AC75 B7                   or      a
 868+ AC76 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AC79 B7                   or      a
 870+ AC7A C9                   ret
 871+ AC7B
 872+ AC7B              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AC7B CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AC7E              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AC7E 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AC81 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AC84              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AC84 37                   scf
 879+ AC85 C9                   ret

 892+ AC91 ED 79                out     (c),a

 894+ AC94
 895+ AC94              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AC94              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AC94              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AC94              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AC94 F5                   push    af
 900+ AC95 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AC96 D5                   push    de
 902+ AC97 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AC98 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AC9A 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify

 906+ AC9F 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ ACA1              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ ACA1 21 F4 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ ACA4              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:

 911+ ACA5 B7                   or      a
 912+ ACA6 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
